## 视图

| 视图类别 | 视图名称 | 视图描述 | SQL 语句 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 系统 | V_SYS_User | 用户 | SELECT dbo. SYS_User.\*, dbo. BT_Hospital. Code AS HospitalCode, dbo. BT_Hospital. CName AS HospitalName, dbo. SYS_Language. Code AS LanguageCode, dbo. SYS_Language. CName AS LanguageName FROM dbo. SYS_User LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. BT_Hospital ON dbo. SYS_User. HospitalDR = dbo. BT_Hospital. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Language ON dbo. SYS_User. LanguageDR = dbo. SYS_Language. RowID ORDER BY dbo. SYS_User. Sequence |
| 系统 | V_SYS_MenuFunction | 系统菜单功能 | SELECT dbo. SYS_MenuFunction.\*, dbo. SYS_Menu. Code AS MenuCode, dbo. SYS_Menu. CName AS MenuName, dbo. SYS_Function. Code AS FunctionCode, dbo. SYS_Function. CName AS FunctionName FROM dbo. SYS_MenuFunction LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Function ON dbo. SYS_MenuFunction. FunctionDR = dbo. SYS_Function. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Menu ON dbo. SYS_MenuFunction. MenuDR = dbo. SYS_Menu. RowID |
| 系统 | V_SYS_GroupMenu | 安全组菜单 | SELECT dbo. SYS_GroupMenu.\*, dbo. SYS_Menu. Code AS MenuCode, dbo. SYS_Menu. CName AS MenuName, dbo. SYS_Group. Code AS GroupCode, dbo. SYS_Group. CName AS GroupName FROM dbo. SYS_GroupMenu LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Group ON dbo. SYS_GroupMenu. GroupDR = dbo. SYS_Group. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Menu ON dbo. SYS_GroupMenu. MenuDR = dbo. SYS_Menu. RowID |
| 系统 | V_SYS_GroupMenuFunction | 安全组菜单功能 | SELECT dbo. SYS_GroupMenuFunction.\*, dbo. V_SYS_GroupMenu.\*, dbo. SYS_Function. Code AS FunctionCode, dbo. SYS_Function. CName AS FunctionName FROM dbo. SYS_GroupMenuFunction LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. V_SYS_GroupMenu ON dbo. SYS_GroupMenuFunction. GroupMenuDR = dbo. V_SYS_GroupMenu. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Function ON dbo. SYS_GroupMenuFunction. FunctionDR = dbo. SYS_Function. RowID |
| 系统 | V_SYS_UserGroup | 安全组 | SELECT dbo. SYS_UserGroup.\*, dbo. SYS_Group. Code AS GroupCode, dbo. SYS_Group. CName AS GroupName, dbo. SYS_User. Code AS UserCode, dbo. SYS_User. CName AS UserName, dbo. BT_WorkGroup. Code AS WorkGroupCode, dbo. BT_WorkGroup. CName AS WorkGroupName FROM dbo. SYS_UserGroup LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Group ON dbo. SYS_UserGroup. GroupDR = dbo. SYS_Group. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_User ON dbo. SYS_UserGroup. UserDR = dbo. SYS_User. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. BT_WorkGroup ON dbo. SYS_UserGroup. WorkGroupDR = dbo. BT_WorkGroup. RowID ORDER BY dbo. SYS_UserGroup. Sequence |
| 系统 | V_SYS_UserLocation | 用户访问科室 | SELECT dbo. SYS_UserLocation.\*, dbo. SYS_Group. Code AS GroupCode, dbo. SYS_Group. CName AS GroupName, dbo. SYS_User. Code AS UserCode, dbo. SYS_User. CName AS UserName, dbo. BT_Location. Code AS LocationCode, dbo. BT_Location. CName AS LocationName FROM dbo. SYS_UserLocation LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. SYS_Group ON dbo. SYS_UserLocation. GroupDR = dbo. OrganismGroupDR = dbo. BT_OrganismGroup. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. BT_Antibiotics ON dbo. BT_OrganismGroupAntibiotics. AntibioticsDR = dbo. BT_Antibiotics. RowID ORDER BY dbo. BT_OrganismGroupAntibiotics. Sequence | |

BT_LocationClass ON dbo. BT_Location. LocationTypeDR = dbo. BT_LocationClass. RowID ORDER BY dbo. BT_Location. Sequence | |

Code AS MaterialCode, dbo. BTQC_Material. CName AS MaterialName, dbo. BTQC_MaterialLevel. CName AS LevelNoName FROM dbo. BTQC_MachineQC LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. BTMI_MachineParameter ON dbo. BTQC_MachineQC. MachineParameterDR = dbo. BTMI_MachineParameter. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. BTQC_Material ON dbo. BTQC_MachineQC. MaterialDR = dbo. BTQC_Material. RowID LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo. ## 血袋状态

| 事件 | PackStatusDR（血袋状态）前状态 | PackStatusDR（血袋状态）后状态 | PackStatusStockStatus（在库状态）前状态 | PackStatusStockStatus（在库状态）后状态【在库可用，在库不可用用，出库】|
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 血袋入库 | 空 | 待入库 | 空 | 库存不可用 |
| 血袋入库审核 | 待入库 | 库存 | 库存不可用 | 库存可用 |
| 返回血站 1 | 待入库 | 出库 | 库存不可用 | 出库 |
| 返回血站 2 | 库存 | 出库 | 库存可用 | 出库 |
| 血袋报废 | 库存 | 报废 | 库存可用 | 出库 |
| 审核配血计划 | 库存 | 待输用 | 库存可用 | 库存不可用 |
| 审核配血计划 | 库存 | 待输用 | 库存可用 | 库存可用 |
| 取消审核配血计划 | 待输用 | 库存 | 库存不可用 | 库存可用 |
| 取消审核配血计划 | 待输用 | 库存 | 库存可用 | 库存可用 |
| 发血 | 待输用 | 输用 | 库存不可用 | 出库 |
| 发血 | 待输用 | 输用 | 库存可用 | 出库 |
| 取消发血 | 输用 | 待输用 | 出库 | 库存不可用 |
| 取消发血 | 输用 | 待输用 | 出库 | 库存可用 |
| 退血 | 输用 | 库存 | 出库 | 库存可用 |
| 血袋回收 | 出库 | 回收 | 出库 | 出库 |
| （入库，分装，配血，发血，修改，处理）NNNNNN | | | | |

## 血库相关枚举

| 字段名称 | 字段代码 | 所属表 | 枚举数据 | 说明 | 备注 | 提出问题 | 推荐的问题解决方案 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 输血方式 | TransfusionMode | BD_AgreeForm 输血知情同意书 | N 异体，A 自体 | 10 异体 Normal，20 自体 Self | 已创建 | | |
| 状态 | Status | BD_AgreeForm | C 未签，A 已签，R 拒签 | 10 未签 Create，20 已签 Sign，30 拒签 Reject | 已创建 | | |
| 有效范围 | EffectRange | BD_AgreeForm | 本次有效 本次就诊有效 | 10 本次有效 EffectThis,20 本次就诊有效 EffectAll | 已创建 | | |
| | | | | | | | |
| 操作类型 | OperateType | BD_ReqFormOperateRecord 输血申请单操作记录 | 创建，作废，审核，取消审核，审批，拒审，接收，取消接收，拒收 | 10 创建 Create，15 作废 Discard，20 审核 Auth，25 取消审核 Cancel，30 审批 Approve，35 拒审 Unapprove，40 接收 Receive，42 取消接收，45 拒收 Reject | 已创建 | | |
| | | | | | | | |
| 心功能评估 | AssHeart | BD_ReqForm 输血申请单 | NYHA 分级-ⅠⅡⅢⅣ；AHA 分级-ABC | NYHA 分级-ⅠⅡⅢⅣ 10,15,20,25；AHA 分级-ABC 10,15,20 | 已创建 | 这种分级应该只能定义出一种来，不然都加载出来会让人觉得有问题 | 分别创建两个枚举常量类 |
| 肝功能评估 | AssLiver | BD_ReqForm | Child-ABC；Child-Pugh；武汉会议分级-ⅠⅡⅢ | Child-ABC 10A 级，15B 级，20C 级；Child-Pugh；武汉会议分级-ⅠⅡⅢ 10 一级 One,15 二级 Tow,20 三级 Three | 已创建 | 这种分级应该只能定义出一种来，不然都加载出来会让人觉得有问题 | 分别创建两个枚举常量类 |
